# Skills · S · part 24

885 entries, session-doc to session-start. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **session-doc**: Produce the narrative session document paired with `session-handoff` — why this happened, what was done, decisions worth revisiting, lessons, next moves. Audience is a human collaborator catching up on the session, not the resuming agent. Invoke after a handoff when the project declares a `## Session docs` destination in `CLAUDE.md`. Also fires on 'write session doc', 'session narrative', 'document this session'. Suggest proactively at session wrap-up if a destination is declared and no doc was produced. Skip when no destination is declared and the user didn't ask. ([ymendel/dotclaude](https://github.com/ymendel/dotclaude/tree/HEAD/skills/session-doc) · ★1)
- **session-doctor**: Diagnose Claude Session Guard health — check that the transcript archive is in sync with live sessions, detect whether anything is deleting or truncating Claude Code session data, verify the usage database, and restore missing sessions from the archive. Use when the user suspects session data or usage/token history is being lost, pruned, or corrupted, when sessions have disappeared from the resume list, or when they ask whether their Claude Code data is safe. ([FiredMosquito831/claude-session-guard](https://github.com/FiredMosquito831/claude-session-guard/tree/HEAD/skills/session-doctor))
